Benediction_initializer = {
	class = "rl_starting_stars"
	init_effect = {
		#generate_home_system_resources = no
	}
	flags = { Imperium_gos }
	
	#asteroid_belt = {
	#	type = rocky_asteroid_belt
	#	radius = 60
	#}
	
	
	planet = {
		class = star
		orbit_distance = 0
		init_effect = {
			prevent_anomaly = yes
		}
	}

	planet = {
		class = "pc_molten"

		orbit_distance = 35
		orbit_angle = { min = 90 max = 270 }
		size = { min = 15 max = 25 }
		has_ring = no

		home_planet = no
	}
	
	planet = {
		class = "pc_toxic"

		orbit_distance = 45
		orbit_angle = { min = 90 max = 270 }
		size = { min = 15 max = 25 }
		has_ring = no
		change_orbit = 4
		
		
		#init_effect = {
		#	prevent_anomaly = yes
		#} 
	}

	planet = {
		class = "pc_barren"

		orbit_distance = 45
		orbit_angle = { min = 90 max = 270 }
		size = { min = 15 max = 25 }
		has_ring = no

		home_planet = no
	}
	
	planet = {
		class = "pc_barren_cold"

		orbit_distance = 45
		orbit_angle = { min = 90 max = 270 }
		size = { min = 15 max = 25 }
		has_ring = no

		home_planet = no
	}

	planet = {
		class = "pc_gas_giant"
